{D555}]@##/_/rgya yi skad du/_tA shin kyin kwang mya'u tsa'i shin wang kyin/_bod skad du/_'phags pa gser 'od dam pa mchog tu rnam par rgyal ba'i mdo sde'i rgyal po theg pa chen po'i mdo/_bam po dang po/_sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' thams cad la phyag 'tshal 
[\[19b.2\]]lo/_/'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus gcig na/_bcom ldan 'das rgyal po'i khab bya rgod spungs pa'i ri shin tu rnam par dag pa chos kyi dbyings zab mo sangs rgyas thams cad kyi spyod yul de bzhin gshegs pa'i gnas na dge slong chen po'i dge 'dun khri phrag dgu khri brgyad stong dang 
[\[19b.3\]]thabs cig tu bzhugs te/_thams cad kyang dgra bcom pa sha stag_/glang po che'i rgyal po ltar shin tu rnam par byang ba sha stag_/zag pa zad pa sha stag_/nyon mongs pa med pa sha stag_/sems shin tu rnam par grol ba sha stag_/shes rab shin tu rnam par grol ba sha stag_/bya 
[\[19b.4\]]ba byas pa sha stag_/khur bor ba sha stag_/bdag gi don rab tu rnyed pa sha stag_/srid pa'i sbyor ba yongs su zad pa sha stag_/dam pa'i dbang mchog thob pa sha stag_/tshul khrims rnam par dag pa la gnas pa sha stag_/thabs dang shes rab tu ldan par mkhas pa'i rgyan gyis 
[\[19b.5\]]brgyan pa sha stag_/rnam par thar pa brgyad dang ldan pa sha stag_/pha rol du yongs su phyin pa sha stag ste/_de dag gi ming la 'di lta ste/_tshe dang ldan pa kun shes kauN+Di n+ya dang /_tshe dang ldan pa rta thul dang /_tshe dang ldan pa rlangs pa dang /_tshe dang ldan pa ming chen dang /_tshe dang ldan 
[\[19b.6\]]pa bzang po dang /_tshe dang ldan pa 'od srung chen po dang /_tshe dang ldan pa lteng rgyas 'od srung dang /_tshe dang ldan pa ga yA 'od srung dang /_tshe dang ldan pa chu bo 'od srung dang /_tshe dang ldan pa shA ri'i bu dang /_tshe dang ldan pa maud gal gyi bu chen po dang /_de la gcig ma gtogs pa 
[\[19b.7\]]de ni tshe dang ldan pa kun dga' bo ste/_de la sogs te nyan thos kyi dge 'dun chen po rnams phyi dro'i dus na so so'i ting nge 'dzin las langs nas bcom ldan 'das gang na pa der lhags te/_bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs gnyis la spyi bos phyag byas nas/_g.yas phyogs nas lan 
[\[20a\]]
[\[20a.1\]]gsum bskor ba byas te phyogs gcig tu 'khod do/_/gzhan yang 'di lta ste/_byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong snyed kyang de na 'dus par gyur te/_thams cad kyang klu'i rgyal po chen po ltar gzi brjid kyi byin gyi mthu che ba/_shin du 
[\[20a.2\]]grags pa dang ldan pa/_cang shes dang ldan pa/_sbyin pa dang tshul khrims yongs su dag pa mchod pa dang /_rim gro byed pa dang /_yongs su dag pa/_bskal pa grangs med pa nas bzod pa dang /_brtson 'grus su ldan pa/_bsam gtan thams cad las yongs su 'das pa/_
[\[20a.3\]]dran pa mngon par gzhag pa/_shes rab kyi sgo yongs su 'byed pa/_rnal 'byor dang thabs dang dbang gis rnam par rol ba/_mngon par shes pa dang /_gzungs dang ldan pa/_spobs pa yongs su mi zad pa nyon mongs pa thams cad yongs su spangs pa/_nyon mongs pa'i bshal 
[\[20a.4\]]thag yongs su gcod pa/_ring po mi thogs par thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes dang ldan par 'gyur ba/_bdud dang /_bdud kyi dmag yongs su 'joms pa/_chos kyi rnga rab tu sgrogs pa/_mu stegs thams cad yongs su zlog pas bsam pa rnam par dag par sbyor ba/_dam 
[\[20a.5\]]pa'i chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba/_lha dang mi thams cad yongs su sgrol ba/_phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rgyan yongs su sgrub pa/_rgyud drug gi skye bo yongs la phan 'dogs pa/_ye shes chen po phun sum tshogs pa/_bzod pa chen po dang yongs su ldan pa/_byams pa 
[\[20a.6\]]chen po dang /_snying rje chen po dang ldan pa'i sems la gnas pa/_gzhan gyis mi thub pa'i stobs chen po dang ldan pa/_sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad du rgyu zhing mchod pa byed pa/_yongs su mya ngan las 'da' bar mi 'jug pa/_ma 'ongs pa'i bskal pa'i mthar yongs su mi zad pa'i 
[\[20a.7\]]yi dam gyi smon lam chen po bskyed pa/_sangs rgyas mang po las shin du zab mo'i rgyu rnam par dag pa bskyed pa/_dus gsum gyi chos ma skyes pa'i bzod pa thob pa/_nyan thos dang /_rang sangs rgyas kyi spyod yul las yongs su 'das pa/_'jig rten gyi don sbyor ba la mkhas pa/_
[\[20b\]]
[\[20b.1\]]ston pa chen pos dgongs te gsungs pa'i chos shin du bkod pa rgya cher ston pa/_stong pa'i mtshan nyid rnam par dag pa'i zab mo la mkhas pa/_the tshom yongs su spangs pa sha stag ste/_de dag gi ming la 'di lta ste/_bsgribs pa med pa'i chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba'i byang chub sems 
[\[20b.2\]]dpa' dang /_chos kyi 'khor lo yongs su bskor bar sems bskyed pa'i byang chub sems dpa' dang /_brtson 'grus yongs su ldan pa'i byang chub sems dpa' dang /_yongs su mi skyo ba'i byang chub sems dpa' dang /_byams pa'i byang chub sems dpa' dang /_'jam dpal byang chub 
[\[20b.3\]]sems dpa' dang /_spyan ras gzigs byang chub sems dpa' dang /_gzungs kyi dbang po'i rgyal po byang chub sems dpa' dang /_spobs pa chen po'i rgyan gyi rgyal po byang chub sems dpa' dang /_ri rab mchog gi rgyal po byang chub sems dpa' dang /_rgya mtsho chen po zab mo'i 
[\[20b.4\]]rgyal po byang chub sems dpa' dang /_rin po che'i rgyal mtshan byang chub sems dpa' dang /_rin po che'i rgyal mtshan chen po'i byang chub sems dpa' dang /_sa'i snying po'i byang chub sems dpa' dang /_nam mkha'i snying po'i byang chub sems dpa' dang /_rin po che'i phyag dbang 
[\[20b.5\]]byang chub sems dpa' dang /_rdo rje'i thal mo byang chub sems dpa' dang /_dga' ba'i stobs byang chub sems dpa' dang /_chos kyi stobs byang chub sems dpa' dang /_'od rgyan chen po byang chub sems dpa' dang /_gser gyi 'od rgyan chen po byang chub sems dpa' dang /_tshul 
[\[20b.6\]]khrims rnam par dag pa'i byang chub sems dpa' dang /_rtag tu ting nge 'dzin byang chub sems dpa' dang /_shes rab rnam par dag pa'i byang chub sems dpa' dang /_brtson 'grus dka' thub byang chub sems dpa' dang /_bsam pa nam mkha' ci bzhin byang chub sems dpa' dang /_
[\[20b.7\]]smon lam chen po yongs su mi gcod pa'i byang chub sems dpa' dang /_sman gtong byang chub sems dpa' dang /_nyon mongs pa'i nad rnam par sel ba'i byang chub sems dpa' dang /_sman gyi rgyal po byang chub sems dpa' dang /_'phags par dga' ba'i byang chub sems dpa' 
[\[21a\]]
[\[21a.1\]]dang /_gong du lung bstan pa thob pa'i byang chub sems dpa' dang /_sprin chen 'od gtsang byang chub sems dpa' dang /_sprin chen chos skyong byang chub sems dpa' dang /_sprin chen grags can dga' byang chub sems dpa' dang /_sprin chen mtha' yas grags byang chub 
[\[21a.2\]]sems dpa' dang /_sprin chen seng ge sgra byang chub sems dpa' dang /_sprin chen gleng po che'i rgyal po'i sgra byang chub sems dpa' dang /_sprin chen bkra shis byang chub sems dpa' dang /_sprin chen rin chen yon tan byang chub sems dpa' dang /_sprin chen nyi ma'i snying po byang chub 
[\[21a.3\]]sems dpa' dang /_sprin chen zla ba'i snying po byang chub sems dpa' dang /_sprin chen skar ma'i 'od byang chub sems dpa' dang /_sprin chen me 'od byang chub sems dpa' dang /_sprin chen glog 'od byang chub sems dpa' dang /_sprin chen 'brug sgra byang chub sems dpa' dang /_sprin chen 
[\[21a.4\]]shes rab char yongs su snyoms pa'i byang chub sems dpa' dang /_sprin chen char gyi rgyal po yongs su dag pa'i byang chub sems dpa' dang /_sprin chen me tog sdong po'i rgyal po byang chub sems dpa' dang /_sprin chen pad+ma sngon po'i bsung byang chub sems dpa' dang /_sprin rin po che'i tsan+dan 
[\[21a.5\]]sku rnam par bsil ba byang chub sems dpa' dang /_sprin chen mun pa rnam par sel ba'i byang chub sems dpa' dang /_sprin chen lta ba'i rab rib rnam par sel ba'i byang chub sems dpa' dang /_byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po'i 'khor de dag phyi dro'i dus na so so'i ting 
[\[21a.6\]]nge 'dzin las langs nas bcom ldan 'das gang na ba der lhags te/_bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs gnyis la spyi bos phyag byas nas g.yas phyogs nas lan gsum du bskor ba byas te phyogs gcig tu 'khod do/_/gzhan yang 'di lta ste/_lits+tsha bI gzhon nu 'bum phrag 'bum brgyad 
[\[21a.7\]]stong yang der 'dus par gyur te/_de dag gi ming la gzhon nu seng ge'i 'od dang /_gzhon nu seng ge'i blo gros dang /_gzhon nu chos byin dang /_gzhon nu dbang po sbyin dang /_gzhon nu 'od chen dang /_gzhon nu dpal chen dang /_gzhon nu sangs rgyas skyabs dang /_gzhon nu chos 
[\[21b\]]
[\[21b.1\]]skyabs dang /_gzhon nu dge 'dun skyabs dang /_gzhon nu rdo rje skyabs dang /_gzhon nu nam mkha' skyabs dang /_gzhon nu nam mkha' sgra sgrogs dang /_gzhon nu rin po che'i snying po dang /_gzhon nu bkra shis mchog gi snying po dang /_de dag gzhon nu thams cad kyi gtso bor 
[\[21b.2\]]gyur nas/_bla na med pa'i byang chub tu gnas te theg pa chen po'i dad pa so sor rnyed nas/_phyi dro'i dus na bcom ldan 'das gang na ba der lhags te/_bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs gnyis la spyi bos phyag byas nas/_g.yas phyogs su lan gsum bskor ba byas nas/_phyogs 
[\[21b.3\]]gcig tu 'khod do/_/gzhan yang 'di lta ste/_lha'i sras po bzhi khri nyis stong yang der 'dus par gyur te/_de dag gi ming la 'di lta ste/_lha'i sras po mthong na dga' ba dang /_lha'i sras po yid dga' ba dang /_lha'i sras po nyi ma'i 'od dang /_lha'i sras po zla ba'i gtsug 
[\[21b.4\]]tor dang /_lha'i sras po nam mkha'i blo gros rnam par dag pa dang /_lha'i sras po nyon mongs pa yongs su spangs pa dang /_lha'i sras po rnam par bkra shis dang /_de dag thams cad kyang lha'i sras po rnams kyi gtso bor gyur pa/_theg pa chen po'i chos yongs su skyong ba'i 
[\[21b.5\]]yi dam chen po'i smon lam 'debs pa/_dam pa'i chos yongs su mi nub par gdung 'tshob pa de dag thams cad phyi dro'i dus na bcom ldan 'das gang na ba der lhags nas bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs gnyis la spyi bos phyag byas nas g.yas phyogs su lan gsum du bskor ba byas 
[\[21b.6\]]nas phyogs gcig tu 'khod do/_/gzhan yang 'di lta ste/_klu'i rgyal po nyi khri brgyad stong yang der 'dus par gyur te/_de dag gi ming la pad+ma'i klu'i rgyal po dang /_e la'i lo ma klu'i rgyal po dang /_stobs po che klu'i rgyal po dang /_sgra bo che klu'i rgyal po dang /_dba' 
[\[21b.7\]]rlabs chung ngu'i klu'i rgyal po dang /_chu'i rgyu 'dzin klu'i rgyal po dang /_gser gyi bzhin klu'i rgyal po dang /_yid bzhin klu'i rgyal po dang /_de dag thams cad kyang klu'i rgyal po'i gtso bor gyur pa/_theg pa chen po'i chos yongs su skyong bar yongs su dga' ba dang /_
[\[22a\]]
[\[22a.1\]]yongs su 'dzin pa dang /_lung nod pa dang /_shin tu zab mo'i bsam pa la mos pa dang /_rjes su yi rangs pa dang /_rnam par skyong zhing 'tsho bar byed de/_phyi dro'i dus na bcom ldan 'das gang na ba der lhags te/_bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs gnyis la 
[\[22a.2\]]spyi bos phyag byas nas g.yas phyogs nas lan gsum du bskor ba byas te/_phyogs gcig tu 'khod do/_/gzhan yang 'di lta ste/_gnod sbyin gyi 'khor sum khri drug stong yang der 'dus par gyur to/_/de dag gi gtso bo thog mar lha'i rgyal po mig mi bzang gis drangs nas a mra'i 
[\[22a.3\]]gnod sbyin dang /_a mra thogs pa'i gnod sbyin dang /_pad+ma 'od kyi snying po'i gnod sbyin dang /_pad+ma'i bzhin gnod sbyin dang /_khro gnyer gnod sbyin dang /_'jigs pa mngon du ston pa'i gnod sbyin dang /_sa kun g.yo ba'i gnod sbyin dang /_
[\[22a.4\]]zas kyi ril ming gnod sbyin dang /_de dag thams cad kyang gnod sbyin gyi gtso bor gyur pa sha stag ste/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i dam pa'i chos la shin tu dad pa'i sems bskyed pa/_dam pa'i chos yongs su skyong zhing srung ba'i sems bskyed pa mi skyo ba/_phyi dro'i dus na bcom ldan 'das gang na ba der lhags 
[\[22a.5\]]te/_bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs gnyis pa spyi bos phyag byas te g.yas phyogs nas lan gsum du bskor ba byas nas phyogs gcig tu 'khod do/_/gzhan yang 'di lta ste/_nam mkha' lding gi 'khor bzhi khri dgu stong yang der 'dus par gyur te/_nam mkha' lding gi rgyal po 
[\[22a.6\]]spos kyi glang po che'i byin gyi mthu dang ldan pas gtso bor gyur pa dang /_gzhan yang 'di lta ste/_dri za'i 'khor dang /_lha ma yin gyi 'khor dang /_mi'am ci'i 'khor dang /_grul bum dang /_lto 'phye chen po'i 'khor dang /_ri dang /_nags tshal dang /_chu bo dang /_rgya mtsho 
[\[22a.7\]]rnams kyi lha dang /_drang srong dang /_yul khams chen po thams cad kyi rgyal po dang /_pho brang 'khor dang /_btsun mo dang /_slas dang /_dad pa rnam par bskyed pa'i bu pho dang /_bu mo dang /_lha dang /_mi la sogs pa yongs su 'dus shing lhags nas thams 
[\[22b\]]
[\[22b.1\]]cad kyang bla na med pa theg pa chen po'i chos 'di yongs su klog pa dang /_'don pa dang /_'chang ba dang /_'dzin pa dang /_yi ger 'dri ba dang /_kun du spel zhing bskyed pa dang /_yongs su 'tsho ba dang /_skyong ba'i dam tshig bcas te/_phyi dro'i dus na bcom ldan 'das 
[\[22b.2\]]gang na ba der lhags te bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs gnyis la spyi bos phyag byas nas g.yas phyogs nas lan gsum bskor ba byas nas phyogs gcig tu 'khod do/_/de ltar nyan thos dang /_byang chub sems dpa' dang /_lha dang /_mi dang /_klu dang /_gnod sbyin la 
[\[22b.3\]]sogs pa thams cad 'dus par gyur nas so so nas bsti stang dang /_rim gro'i sems bskyed de/_thal mo sbyar nas rab tu 'dzum pa'i mig gis bcom ldan 'das la blta zhing cung zad kyang mi yengs par dam pa'i chos shin tu thos par smon to/_/de nas bcom ldan 
[\[22b.4\]]'das phyi dro'i dus kyi tshe ting nge 'dzin las bzhengs nas 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor la gzigs te tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag gsungs so/_/'phags pa gser 'od dam pa'i chos/_/mdo sde kun gyi rgyal po 'di/_/sangs rgyas kun gyi spyod yul te/_/shin tu mnyan cing 
[\[22b.5\]]thos par dka'/_/ngas deng dkyil 'khor khyed rnams la/_/dam pa'i mdo sde 'di bstan pas/_/phyogs bzhi sangs rgyas rnams kyis kyang /_/byin gyi rlabs kyis brlabs par 'gyur/_/shar phyogs sangs rgyas mi 'khrugs dang /_/lho phyogs sangs 
[\[22b.6\]]rgyas dkon mchog dpal/_/nub phyogs sangs rgyas snang mtha' yas/_/byang phyogs sangs rgyas rnga sgra skyabs/_/ngas deng chos mchog 'di bstan pas/_/bkra shis 'chags pa'i dam pa ste/_/sdig pa thams cad gzhom pa'i phyir/_/sdig pa'i las kun 
[\[22b.7\]]zad par 'gyur/_/sdug bsngal thams cad 'joms par byed/_/bde ba thams cad sbyin byed cing /_/ye shes rtsa ba kun mkhyen dang /_/dpal kun gyis kyang brgyan par 'gyur/_/sems can gang dag dbang po nyams/_/dpal med pas ni gang zin cing /_/lha 
[\[23a\]]
[\[23a.1\]]rnams kyang ni 'phang bar 'gyur/_/gnyen sdug kun kyang sdang ba dang /_/bu smad 'khor dang bral ba dang /_/phan tshun du ni mi 'phrod cing /_/nor stor bas ni rab gtses te/_/gza' dang skar mas gnod byas shing /_/byad stems mi bzad zin pa dang /_/mya 
[\[23a.2\]]ngan nyon mongs las rnams kyis/_/shin tu mi bzad gzir ba dang /_/rmi lam ltas sdig mthong ba las/_/mya ngan rab tu 'khrug pa rnams/_/gtsang mar lus kyis khrus gyis la/_/gos gtsang sar pa de bgos nas/_/mdo sde dam pa'i rgyal po 'di/_/sangs rgyas 
[\[23a.3\]]zab mo'i spyod yul te/_/klog 'don rab tu 'dzin pa la/_/gus pa'i sems kyis nyon cig dang /_/mdo sde 'di yi byin brlabs kyis/_/rmi ngan ltas sdig 'joms pa dang /_/sdug bsngal thams cad bzlog pas ni/_/yongs su ma lus zad par 'gyur/_/'jig rten 
[\[23a.4\]]skyong ba'i rgyal po bzhi/_/blon po dang ni 'khor bcas dang /_/gnod sbyin bye ba mang po rnams/_/shin tu gus pas rim gro bskyed/_/spobs pa'i lha mo chen mo dang /_/nai ra ny+dza na ra gnas pa'i lha/_/de bzhin 'phrog ma 'byung po'i ma/_/sra ba sa yi lha mo dang /_/tshangs 
[\[23a.5\]]pa'i dbang po brgya byin dang /_/klu yi rgyal po mi'am ci/_/nam mkha' lding gi rgyal po dang /_/lha ma yin gyi 'khor rnams bcas/_/de ltar mthu chen de dag kun/_/rang gi 'khor rnams khrid nas ni/_/nyin mtshan rtag tu mi 'bral bar/_/gang zag de la srung bar byed/_/
[\[23a.6\]]zab mo sangs rgyas spyod yul dang /_/sangs rgyas dgongs te gsungs pa'i bka'/_/mdo sde gang yin ngas bshad pa/_/bskal pa bye bar phrad dka' bas/_/gang gis mdo sde 'di thos nas/_/gzhan la rgya cher ston pa dang /_/gal te sems kyis yi rangs dang /_/mchod 
[\[23a.7\]]pa bshams nas mchod byed na/_/de 'dra'i gang zag de dag ni/_/bskal pa tshad med snyed du yang /_/rtag par lha dang mi rnams dang /_/klu la sogs pas mchod bkur 'gyur/_/'di yi bsod nams tshad med pas/_/gang gA'i bye snyed grangs las 'das/_/mdo sde 'di ni gang 
[\[23b\]]
[\[23b.1\]]klog pa/_/yon tan 'di dag thob par 'gyur/_/phyogs bcu'i bcom ldan 'das rnams dang /_/spyod zab byang chub sems dpa' rnams/_/mdo sde 'dzin la yongs bsrungs nas/_/sdug bsngal bgegs dang bral bar mdzad/_/mdo sde 'di ni gang mchod pas/_/snga 
[\[23b.2\]]ma bzhin du lus bkrus nas/_/zas dang me tog spos mchod cing /_/byams dang snying rje'i sems bskyed de/_/mdo sde 'di ni nyan 'dod pas/_/sems gtsang dri ma med byas te/_/rtag par dga' ba'i sems bskyed na/_/yon tan mang po skye bar 'gyur/_/gang zhig 
[\[23b.3\]]gus pa'i sems dag gis/_/mdo sde 'di ni nyan byed na/_/legs par mi yi 'gror skye zhing /_/sdug bsngal bgegs dang 'bral bar 'gyur/_/de ni dge rtsa smin pas na/_/sangs rgyas rnams kyis bsngags pa yi/_/mdo sde 'di ni thos pa dang /_/'gyod tshangs chos ni 
[\[23b.4\]]thos pa yin/_/gser 'od dam pa mchog tu rnam par rgyal ba mdo sde'i rgyal po las gleng gzhi'i le'u ste dang po'o//_//de nas de'i tshe rgyal po'i khab kyi grong khyer chen po na/_byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po mdzes pa'i tog ces bya ba 'das pa'i sangs 
[\[23b.5\]]rgyas bye ba phrag brgya stong tshad med pa rnams la bsnyen bkur dang rim gro byas nas dge ba'i rtsa ba bskyed pa zhig yod de/_de'i tshe byang chub sems dpa' mdzes pa'i tog ces bya ba gcig pu dben par gnas pa de 'di snyam du rgyu gang dang rkyen gang gi phyir de bzhin gshegs pa shAkya 
[\[23b.6\]]thub pa sku tshe thung ste lo brgyad cur gyur snyam mo/_/gzhan yang 'di snyam du bcom ldan 'das kyis ji skad gsungs pa ltar na rgyu dang rkyen rnam pa gnyis kyis tshe ring por 'gyur ro/_/zhes gsungs te/_gnyis gang zhe na sems can gyi srog mi gcod pa dang /_gzhan dag la 
[\[23b.7\]]yang bza' ba dang btung ba yongs su btang ba'o/_/de bzhin gshegs pa shAkya thub pa ni 'das pa'i bskal pa chen po bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong grangs med pa snyed du sems can gyi srog ma bcad cing dge ba bcu'i las kyis lam spyod pa dang /_rtag par bza' ba dang /_
[\[24a\]]
[\[24a.1\]]btung ba dag bkres pa'i sems can thams cad la sbyin pa btang ste/_nyid kyi sha dang /_khrag dang /_rus pa dang /_rkang la sogs pa yang yongs su btang nas rab tu tshim par mdzad na/_bza' ba dang /_btung ba gzhan lta smos kyang ci dgos snyam mo/_/byang chub 
[\[24a.2\]]sems dpa' des bcom ldan 'das la de snyam du bsams pa'i tshe/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis byin gyis brlabs kyi mthus de'i khang khyim de ma thag tu yangs shing rgya che ba dang /_bkod pa dang ldan zhing yongs su gtsang ba dang /_bai DUr+ya sngon po dang /_rin po che rnam pa sna tshogs kyis 
[\[24a.3\]]rab tu brgyan pas sangs rgyas kyi zhing ji lta ba bzhin du spos mchog dam pa'i dri lha'i spos las 'das pa dag 'thul zhing gang ba dang /_phyogs bzhin seng ge'i gdan khri mchog dam pa rin po che sna bzhi las mngon par grub pa/_lha'i rin po che'i gos kyis g.yogs pa dang /_gzhan 
[\[24a.4\]]yang gdan de la pad+ma dam pa rin po che sna tshogs kyis brgyan pa tshad de bzhin gshegs pa dang mnyam pa dag rang gi ngang gis snang bar gyur te/_pad+ma de dag gi steng na de bzhin gshegs pa bzhi po shar phyogs kyi mi 'khrugs pa dang /_lho phyogs kyi rin chen mtshan dang /_nub

[\[24a.6\]]gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'di dang /_phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing gang gA'i klung gi bye ma dang mnyam pa snyed kyi bar du yang snang bas kun tu khyab par mdzad de/_lha'i me tog gi char yang mngon par phab lha'i rol mo'i sil snyan rnams kyang dkrol bar gyur te/_
[\[24a.7\]]de'i tshe 'dzam bu'i gling 'di dang stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'di na/_sems can ji snyed yod pa de dag bcom ldan 'das kyis byin gyis brlabs kyi mthus bde ba mchog dam pa myong bar gyur pas phongs pa ci yang med par gyur to/_/lus kyi yan lag 
[\[24b\]]
[\[24b.1\]]ma tshang ba dag ni tshang bar gyur/_long ba dag ni mthong bar gyur/_'on pa rnams ni thos par gyur/_lkugs pa dag ni smra bar gyur/_glen pa dag ni shes rab dang ldan par gyur/_sems 'khrugs pa dag ni sems nyid thob par gyur/_bgo ba med pa dag ni gos dang ldan

[\[26b.1\]]par byed la/_yang dag pa'i chos bstan pa thos par gyur na yang dag pa'i bka' yin no snyam pa'i 'du shes bskyed nas/_mdo sde ji snyed pa thams cad kyang yang dag par 'dzin cing skur pa 'debs par mi byed do/_/rigs kyi bu rgyu dang rkyen des na bcom ldan 'das de yun ring

[\[27b.1\]]'das kyis yongs su mya ngan las 'da' bar gshegs pa bkas bshad pa thos pa dang mchi ma blags te ngus nas mdun du 'phags te bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la phyag byas nas bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/gal te de bzhin gshegs pa yang dag par

[\[28b.1\]]kyi/_/bcom ldan 'das kyi ring bsrel ni/_/mthar ni nams kyang dmigs mi 'gyur/_/gal te ru sbal spu las ni/_/gos mchog dam pa rab btags nas/_/grang tshe bgo bar rung 'gyur na/_/bcom ldan ring bsrel btsal bar bya/_/gal te sbrang bu'i rkang pa las/_/khang

[\[28b.6\]]ni byas gyur na/_/bcom ldan ring bsrel btsal bar bya/_/gal te gru chen thams cad las/_/nor dang rin chen rab bkang ste/_/thang bu skam la 'gro nus na/_/bcom ldan ring bsrel btsal bar bya/_/gal te sbrang bu lcags phye mchus/_/spos kyi ri bo khyer nas ni/_/dga' 
[\[28b.7\]]mgur 'phur zhing 'gro gyur na/_/bcom ldan ring bsrel btsal bar bya/_/de nas de'i tshe bram ze chos kyi slob dpon gyis lung bstan pas tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag thos nas gzhon nu sems can thams cad kyis mthong bar dga' ba la tshigs su bcad de smras pa/_legs 
[\[29a\]]
[\[29a.1\]]so gzhon nu chen po khyod/_/'khor 'di kun gyi bkra shis yin/_/thabs la mkhas pa'i sems kyis ni/_/bla med sangs rgyas lung bstan thob/_/de bzhin gshegs pa gzigs chen gyis/_/'jig rten dag ni skyob mdzad pas/_/khyod kyis rtse gcig nyon cig 
[\[29a.2\]]dang /_/deng bdag rim bzhin bshad par bya/_/sangs rgyas spyod yul bsam dka' bas/_/'jig rten dag na mtshungs pa med/_/chos sku ngo bo rtag bzhugs pas/_/rnal 'byor sgom la khyad par med/_/sangs rgyas sku kun mtshungs pas na/_/chos gsungs pa 
[\[29a.3\]]yang de bzhin te/_/sangs rgyas rnams la mdzad mi mnga'/_/gzod ma nyid nas skye ba med/_/bcom ldan rdo rje'i sku yin yang /_/thabs kyis sprul pa'i sku ston pas/_/de phyir bcom ldan ring bsrel ni/_/yungs 'bru tsam yang yod pa min/_/bcom ldan sha 
[\[29a.4\]]khrag sku min na/_/ji ltar ring bsrel yod par 'gyur/_/sems can rnams la phan gdags phyir/_/thabs kyis sku gdung gzhag pa yin/_/chos sku yang dag sangs rgyas pas/_/chos dbyings de bzhin gshegs pa yin/_/yang 
[\[29a.5\]]dag sku ni 'di yin pas/_/de 'dra'i chos ni gsungs par mdzad/_/de nas 'khor 'dus pa de nyid du lha'i bu sum khri nyis stong gis de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku tshe'i tshad shin tu ring bar bstan pa thos nas thams cad bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems bskyed 
[\[29a.6\]]nas ngo mtshar rmad du byung ba'i dga' ba dang /_mgu ba dang /_rangs pa thob pas thams cad mgrin gcig tu tshigs su bcad de smras pa/_sangs rgyas mya ngan mi 'da' ste/_/yang dag chos kyang nub mi 'gyur/_/sems can rnams la phan gdags phyir/_/mya ngan 'da' bar 
[\[29a.7\]]bstan pa yin/_/bcom ldan bsam gyis mi khyab cing /_/sku mchog tha dad mtshan med kyang /_/sems can rnams la phan gdags phyir/_/bkod pa sna tshogs ston par mdzad/_/de nas byang chub sems dpa' mdzes pa'i tog gis bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar de bzhin 
[\[29b\]]
[\[29b.1\]]gshegs pa bzhi dang /_skyes bu chen po de gnyis dang /_lha'i bu rnams las de bzhin gshegs pa shAkya thub pa'i sku tshe'i tshad bstan pa mngon sum du thos pas yang 'dug pa'i stan las langs te thal mo sbyar nas gus pas bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom 
[\[29b.2\]]ldan 'das gal te yang dag par 'di ltar de bzhin gshegs pa rnams yongs su mya ngan las mi 'da' zhing ring bsrel yang mi mnga' ba zhig lags na ci'i slad du mdo sde las yongs su mya ngan las 'da' ba dang /_sangs rgyas la ring bsrel mnga' bar gsungs shing /_lha 
[\[29b.3\]]dang mi rnams kyis rim gro dang /_bkur sti dang /_mchod pa bgyid par 'gyur ba'i ched du 'das pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyis kyang sku gdung mnga' bar bstan cing 'jig rten na bzhugs pas lha dang mi rnams kyis mchod cing rim gro bgyis na bsod nams dpag tu ma 
[\[29b.4\]]mchis pa thob par 'gyur ro/_/zhes gsungs la/_deng ni ma mchis so zhes gsungs pas the tsom dang som nyi 'tshal bar gyur na/_bcom ldan 'das bdag cag la thugs brtse bar dgongs te rgya cher rnam par dbye bar gsol/_de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub 
[\[29b.5\]]sems dpa' mdzes pa'i tog dang /_'khor mang po rnams la bka' stsal pa/_khyod kyis 'di ltar shes par bya ste/_yongs su mya ngan las 'da' zhing ring bsrel yod do/_/zhes gsungs pa ni dgongs pa'i dbang gis gsungs pa yin pas 'di ltar de'i don gang yin 
[\[29b.6\]]pa sems rtse gcig tu nyon cig_/rigs kyi bu byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams kyis 'di ltar chos rnam pa bcus de bzhin gshegs pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi/_de kho na nyid kyi don gyi tshul gyis mthar phyin pa'i mya ngan las 'das pa 
[\[29b.7\]]chen po yod par gsungs pa gang yin pa shes par 'gyur bar rig par bya'o/_/bcu gang zhe na/_dang po ni de bzhin gshegs pa rnams nyon mongs pa'i sgrib pa dang shes bya'i sgrib pa ma lus par spangs shing zad par gyur pa'i phyir yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa zhes bya'o/_/
[\[30a\]]
[\[30a.1\]]gnyis pa ni de bzhin gshegs pa rnams sems can ngo bo nyid med pa dang chos ngo bo nyid med par legs par thugs su chud pa'i phyir yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa zhes bya'o/_/gsum pa ni lus kyi gnas dang chos kyi gnas yongs su gyur pa'i phyir yongs su 
[\[30a.2\]]mya ngan las 'das pa zhes bya'o/_/bzhi pa ni sems can rnams la don mdzad pa'i rgyu dang rkyen rang gi ngang gis nye bar zhi bar gyur pa'i phyir yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa zhes bya'o/_/lnga pa ni de kho na nyid kyi mtshan nyid tha dad pa med pa mnyam pa nyid kyis chos kyi sku mngon sum du brnyes 
[\[30a.3\]]pa'i phyir yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa zhes bya'o/_/drug pa ni 'khor ba dang mya ngan las 'das pa la ngo bo nyid gnyis su med par thugs su chud pa'i phyir yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa zhes bya'o/_/bdun pa ni chos thams cad kyi rtsa ba thugs su chud nas/_
[\[30a.4\]]rnam par dag pa mngon sum du gyur pa'i phyir/_yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa zhes bya'o/_/brgyad pa ni chos thams cad la skye ba med cing 'gag pa med par rnal 'byor legs par bsgoms pa'i phyir/_yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa zhes bya'o/_/dgu pa ni de bzhin nyid dang 
[\[30a.5\]]chos kyi dbyings dang /_yang dag pa'i mtha' mnyam pa nyid kyis yang dag pa'i ye shes brnyes pa'i phyir/_yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa zhes bya'o/_/bcu pa ni chos rnams kyi ngo bo nyid dang mya ngan las 'das pa'i ngo bo nyid la bye brag med pa brnyes pa'i phyir/_yongs su 
[\[30a.6\]]mya ngan las 'das pa zhes bya ste/_rigs kyi bu 'di dag ni yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa yod par bstan pa'i chos rnam pa bcu zhes bya'o/_/gzhan yang rigs kyi bu byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams kyis 'di ltar chos rnam pa bcus de bzhin 
[\[30a.7\]]gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyis de kho na nyid kyi don gyi tshul gyis mthar phyin pa'i yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa chen po yod par gsungs pa gang yin pa shes par 'gyur bar rig par bya'o/_/bcu gang zhe na/_dang po ni nyon mongs pa thams 
[\[30b\]]
[\[30b.1\]]cad ni 'dod pas gzhi byas pa yin pas 'dod pa las skyes pa yin la sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni 'dod pa yongs su bcad pa'i phyir yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa zhes bya'o/_/gnyis pa ni de bzhin gshegs pa rnams nyon mongs pa'i 'dod pa rnams yongs su 
[\[30b.2\]]bcad pas chos 'ga' yang len par mi mdzad la/_len par mi mdzad pa'i phyir gshegs pa yang mi mnga' byon pa yang mi mnga' zhing len pa mi mnga' ba'i phyir yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa zhes bya'o/_/gsum pa ni 'di ltar de bzhin gshegs pa la gshegs pa dang 
[\[30b.3\]]byon pa mi mnga' zhing len pa mi mnga' ba gang yin pa de ni chos kyi sku skye ba med cing 'gag pa med pa yin pas skye ba dang 'gag pa med par gyur pa'i phyir yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa zhes bya'o/_/bzhi pa ni skye ba dang 'gag pa med pa gang yin pa de ni tshig gis brjod par bya ba ma yin te/_
[\[30b.4\]]tshig gi lam yongs su chad par gyur pa'i phyir yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa zhes bya'o/_/lnga pa ni bdag dang gang zag med kyi chos tsam 'ba' zhig skye 'gag tu gyur pa'i gnas su gyur pa brnyes pa'i phyir yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa zhes bya'o/_/drug pa ni nyon mongs pa dang /_nye 
[\[30b.5\]]ba'i nyon mongs pa gang yin pa de dag thams cad ni blo bur gyi rdul yin la chos nyid gang yin pa de ni bdag po yin te/_'gro ba med cing 'ong ba med par sangs rgyas rnams kyis thugs su chud pa'i phyir yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa zhes bya'o/_/bdun pa ni de bzhin nyid gang yin pa 
[\[30b.6\]]de ni yang dag pa yin la gzhan thams cad ni yang dag pa ma yin pa yin pas yang dag pa'i ngo bo nyid gang yin pa de ni de bzhin nyid yin la/_de bzhin nyid kyi ngo bo nyid gang yin pa de ni de bzhin gshegs pa yin pas yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa zhes bya'o/_/brgyad pa ni yang dag 
[\[30b.7\]]pa'i mtha'i ngo bo nyid gang yin pa de la ni spros pa med de/_de bzhin gshegs pa nyag gcig gis yang dag pa'i mtha'i chos mngon sum du gyur nas/_spros pa gtan du bcad pas na/_yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa zhes bya'o/_/dgu pa ni skye ba med pa gang yin 
[\[31a\]]
[\[31a.1\]]pa de ni yang dag pa yin la/_skye ba gang yin pa de ni mi bden pa yin pas byis pa blun po rnams 'khor bar bying zhing 'dod pas khyer ba yin na/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku la ni yang dag par mi bden pa mi mnga' bas yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa zhes bya'o/_/
[\[31a.2\]]bcu pa ni yang dag pa'i chos ma yin pa gang yin pa de ni rkyen las byung ba yin la yang dag pa'i chos gang yin pa de ni rkyen las byung ba ma yin te/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i chos kyi sku'i ngo bo nyid dang yin pa de ni yang dag pa yin pas yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa zhes bya ste/_rigs 
[\[31a.3\]]kyi bu 'di dag ni yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa yod par bstan pa'i chos rnams pa bcu zhes bya'o/_/gzhan yang rigs kyi bu byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams kyis 'di ltar yang chos rnam pa bcus de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs 
[\[31a.4\]]pa'i sangs rgyas kyi de kho na nyid kyi don gyi tshul gyis mthar phyin pa'i mya ngan las 'das pa chen po yod par gsungs pa gang yin pa shes par 'gyur bar rig par bya'o/_/bcu gang zhe na/_dang po ni de bzhin gshegs pas sbyin pa dang sbyin pa'i 'bras bu la bdag dang bdag gi ba 
[\[31a.5\]]med par legs par thugs su chud pas sbyin pa dang sbyin pa'i 'bras bu de la log par rtog pa gtan du bsal nas zhi bar gyur pa'i phyir yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa zhes bya'o/_/gnyis pa ni de bzhin gshegs pas tshul khrims dang tshul khrims kyi 'bras bu la bdag dang 
[\[31a.6\]]bdag gi ba med par legs par thugs su chud pas tshul khrims dang tshul khrims kyi 'bras bu de la log par rtog pa gtan du bsal nas zhi bar gyur pa'i phyir yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa zhes bya'o/_/gsum pa ni de bzhin gshegs pas bzod pa dang bzod pa'i 'bras bu 
[\[31a.7\]]la bdag dang bdag gi ba med par legs par thugs su chud pas bzod pa dang bzod pa'i 'bras bu de la/_/log par rtog par gtan du bsal nas zhi bar gyur pa'i phyir yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa zhes bya'o/_/bzhi pa ni de bzhin gshegs pas brtson 'grus dang /_brtson 
[\[31b\]]
[\[31b.1\]]'grus kyi 'bras bu la bdag dang /_bdag gi ba med par legs par thugs su chud pas brtson 'grus dang brtson 'grus kyi 'bras bu de la log par rtog pa gtan du bsal nas zhi bar gyur pa'i phyir yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa zhes bya'o/_/lnga pa ni de bzhin gshegs 
[\[31b.2\]]pas bsam gtan dang bsam gtan gyi 'bras bu la bdag dang bdag gi ba med par legs par thugs su chud pas bsam gtan dang bsam gtan gyi 'bras bu de la log par rtog pa gtan du bsal nas zhi bar gyur pa'i phyir yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa zhes bya'o/_/
[\[31b.3\]]drug pa ni de bzhin gshegs pas shes rab dang shes rab kyi 'bras bu la bdag dang bdag gi ba med par legs par thugs su chud pas shes rab dang shes rab kyi 'bras bu de la log par rtog pa gtan du bsal nas zhi bar gyur pa'i phyir yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa zhes 
[\[31b.4\]]bya'o/_/bdun pa ni de bzhin gshegs pas sems can dang sems can ma yin pa thams cad dang chos thams cad la ngo bo nyid med par legs par thugs su chud pas log par rtog pa gtan du bsal nas zhi bar gyur pa'i phyir yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa zhes bya'o/_/
[\[31b.5\]]brgyad pa ni gang gis bdag nyid la sred cing mngon par zhen pa des ni yongs su tshol bar byed pa la yongs su tshol ba'i phyir ni sdug bsngal mang po myong par 'gyur ba yin na/_de bzhin gshegs pa rnams ni bdag nyid la sred pa spangs pa'i phyir yongs su tshol ba gtan du 
[\[31b.6\]]bcad de yongs su tshol ba mi mnga' ba'i phyir yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa zhes bya'o/_/dgu pa ni 'dus byas kyi chos thams cad ni grangs dang tshad yod pa yin la 'dus ma byas kyi chos gang yin pa de ni grangs dang tshad spangs pa yin te/_de bzhin gshegs pa ni 'dus byas 
[\[31b.7\]]kyi chos spangs shing 'dus ma byas kyi chos mngon du mdzad pas grangs dang tshad mi mnga' ba'i phyir yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa zhes bya'o/_/bcu pa ni de bzhin gshegs pas sems can dang chos kyi ngo bo nyid thams cad stong zhing stong pa nyid las gud na stong pa'i ngo bo nyid 
[\[32a\]]
[\[32a.1\]]yod pa ma yin pa gang yin pa de nyid la yang dag pa'i chos kyi sku yin par thugs su chud pa'i phyir yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa zhes bya ste/_rigs kyi bu 'di dag ni mya ngan las 'das pa yod par bstan pa'i chos rnam pa bcu zhes bya'o/_/gzhan yang rigs kyi 
[\[32a.2\]]bu de bzhin gshegs pa yongs su mya ngan las mi 'da' ba gang yin pa de yang ci ngo mtshar rmad du byung ba ma yin nam/_gzhan yang ngo mtshar gyi chos rnam pa bcu dang ldan pa gang yin pa de ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i mdzad pa yin te/_bcu gang zhe na/_'khor ba ni nyes dmigs dang 
[\[32a.3\]]bcas pa yin la/_mya ngan las 'das pa ni nye bar zhi ba yin yang 'khor ba dang mya ngan las 'das pa mnyam pa nyid du mkhyen pa'i phyir 'khor bar mi gnas par yang mi 'gyur la/_mya ngan las 'das pa la gnas par yang mi mdzad pas sems can rnams la yongs su skyo ba mi skyed 
[\[32a.4\]]pa gang yin pa de ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i mdzad pa zhes bya'o/_/de bzhin gshegs pa ni sems can rnams la 'di snyam du byis pa so so'i skye bo 'di dag ni phyin ci log gi lta ba la spyod pas nyon mongs pa rnams kyis dkris shing nyon mongs par gyur pas ngas deng rnam par phye ste khong du 
[\[32a.5\]]chud par byas nas rnam par thar bar bya'o snyam du mi dgongs mod kyi/_'on kyang sngon gyi byams pa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba'i mthus sems can de dag gi dbang po dang /_rang bzhin dang /_bsam pa dang /_mos pa ji lta ba bzhin du rnam par rtog pa mi skyed par yang rang gi ngang gis yongs 
[\[32a.6\]]su sgrol bar mdzad cing /_phyi ma'i mthar thug pa'i bar du yang dag pa'i bstan pa bshad nas phan 'dogs shing dga' bar mdzad pa la mthar thug pa med pa gang yin pa de ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i mdzad pa zhes bya'o/_/de bzhin gshegs pa la ngas deng gsung rab yan lag bcu 
[\[32a.7\]]gnyis bstan nas sems can la phan gdags par bya'o snyam pa'i dgongs pa mi mnga' mod kyi/_'on kyang sngon gyi byams pa'i dge ba'i mthus phyi ma'i mthar thug pa'i bar du sems can de dag la rgya cher bstan pa mdzad pa la mthar thug pa med pa gang yin pa de ni de bzhin gshegs 
[\[32b\]]
[\[32b.1\]]pa'i mdzad pa zhes bya'o/_/de bzhin gshegs pa la ngas deng grong dang grong khyer dang /_grong rdal de dag tu song ste rgyal po dang blon po dang bram ze dang /_rgyal rigs dang /_dmangs rigs la sogs pa'i pho brang dang /_khyim dag tu bsod snyoms bslang bar bya'o/_/snyam pa'i 
[\[32b.2\]]dgongs pa mi mnga' mod kyi/_'on kyang sngon sku dang gsung dang thugs kyi spyod pa goms par gyur pa'i mthus rang gi ngang gis de dag tu gshegs nas phan pa'i dngos po mdzad cing bsod snyoms slong ba gang yin pa de ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i mdzad pa zhes bya'o/_/de bzhin 
[\[32b.3\]]gshegs pa'i sku la bkres pa dang /_skom pa dang /_bshang gci dang /_nyam chung ba'i mtshan ma yang mi mnga' la bsod snyoms slong bar mdzad kyang bshos gsol ba mi mnga' zhing rnam par rtog pa yang mi mnga' mod kyi/_'on kyang rang gi ngang gis sems can rnams 
[\[32b.4\]]la phan gdags pa'i phyir bshos gsol ba mnga' ba'i mtshan ma ston pa gang yin pa de ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i mdzad pa zhes bya'o/_/de bzhin gshegs pa la sems can mang po 'di dag la rab dang /_'bring dang /_tha ma yod pa de dag gi dbang po dang rang bzhin ji lta ba 
[\[32b.5\]]bzhin du chos bstan par bya'o snyam pa'i dgongs pa mi mnga' mod kyi/_'on kyang sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnam par rtog pa mi mnga' bar yang de dag gi snod dang dbang po'i skyon ji lta ba bzhin du shin tu 'tsham par de dag la chos ston par mdzad pa gang yin pa de ni de bzhin 
[\[32b.6\]]gshegs pa'i mdzad pa zhes bya'o/_/de bzhin gshegs pa la sems can 'di dag ni nga la bkur sti dang rim gro mi byed cing rtag par nga la smod pa'i tshig 'byin par byed pas de dag dang lhan cig tu gtam smra bar mi bya'o/_/sems can 'di dag ni nga la bkur sti dang rim gro byed 
[\[32b.7\]]cing rtag par nga'i bsngags pa rjod par byed pas ngas de dag dang lhan cig tu gtam smra bar bya'o snyam pa'i dgongs pa mi mnga' mod kyi/_'on kyang de bzhin gshegs pas byams pa dang thugs rje'i dgongs pa mnyam pa bskyed nas gnyis su med par gyur pa gang yin pa de ni 
[\[33a\]]
[\[33a.1\]]de bzhin gshegs pa'i mdzad pa zhes bya'o/_/de bzhin gshegs pa rnams la byams sdang dang /_nga rgyal dang /_'dod pa dang /_ser sna dang /_nyon mongs pa rnams mi mnga' mod kyi/_'on kyang de bzhin gshegs pa rtag par rab tu dben pa la 
[\[33a.2\]]dgyes shing 'dod pa chung ba dang /_'du 'dzi spangs pa la bsngags pa brjod par mdzad pa gang yin pa de ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i mdzad pa zhes bya'o/_/de bzhin gshegs pa ni chos gcig tsam yang mi mkhyen pa'am/_legs par thugs su ma chud pa mi mnga' bas 
[\[33a.3\]]thams cad du me long lta bu'i ye shes mngon sum du gyur cing rnam par mi rtog pa mi mnga' mod kyi/_'on kyang de bzhin gshegs pas sems can de dag gis las kyi dngos po mngon par 'du byed pa gzigs nas de dag gi yid dang mthun par 'jug cing thabs mkhyen pas 
[\[33a.4\]]drangs te nges par 'byung ba thob par mdzad pa gang yin pa de ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i mdzad pa zhes bya'o/_/de bzhin gshegs pa ni gal te sems can kha cig phyug cing longs spyod dang ldan par gyur pa gzigs na yang dgyes pa skye bar mi 'gyur la/_de dag rgud 
[\[33a.5\]]cing phongs par gyur pa gzigs na yang yongs su gdung ba skye bar mi 'gyur mod kyi/_'on kyang de bzhin gshegs pas sems can de dag yang dag pa'i spyod pa mngon par bsgrubs pa gzigs par gyur nas thogs pa mi mnga' ba'i thugs rje chen pos rang gi ngang gis yongs su skyob 
[\[33a.6\]]cing sdud par mdzad pa dang /_gal te sems can de dag log pa'i spyod pa mngon par bsgrub pa gzigs par gyur na thogs pa mi mnga' ba'i thugs rje chen pos rang gi ngang gis yongs su skyob cing sdud par mdzad pa gang yin pa de ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i mdzad pa zhes bya'o/_/rigs 
[\[33a.7\]]kyi bu de ltar na de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas ni de lta bu'i yang dag pa'i mdzad pa dpag tu med pa mnga' bar ston pa yin par rig par bya zhing /_khyed kyis 'di dag ni yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa'i yang dag pa'i mtshan nyid yin par rig 
[\[33b\]]
[\[33b.1\]]par bya'o/_/res 'ga' yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa yod par mthong ba gang yin pa de yang thabs mkhyen pas bstan pa yin la ring bsrel bzhag nas sems can rnams kyis bkur sti dang rim gro byas te mchod pa byed par 'gyur ba yang de bzhin gshegs pa'i byams 
[\[33b.2\]]pa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba'i mthu las gyur pa yin pas gang gis mchod pa byas pa de dag ma 'ongs pa'i dus na mi khom pa brgyad dang bral zhing de bzhin gshegs pa rnams la bsnyen bkur byed pa dang /_dge ba'i bshes gnyen dang phrad nas dge ba'i sems stor bar mi 'gyur 
[\[33b.3\]]ba dang /_bsod nams kyi rnam par smin pa dpag tu med pa dang ldan zhing myur du nges par byung nas 'khor ba dag gis blangs shing 'ching bar mi 'gyur bas 'di lta bu'i spyod pa gya nom pa gang yin pa de khyod kyis nan tan gyis bsgrub par bya zhing bag med par mi bya'o/_/
[\[33b.4\]]de nas byang chub sems dpa' mdzes pa'i tog gis bcom ldan 'das la mngon sum du yongs su mya ngan las mi 'da' ba dang /_shin tu zab pa'i mdzad pa bkas bshad pa thos nas gus pas thal mo sbyar nas 'di skad ces bdag ni deng gdod de bzhin gshegs pa

[\[34a.1\]]le'u ste gnyis pa'o//_//gser 'od dam pa mchog tu rnam par rgyal ba [(]mdo [,]mdo [)]sde'i rgyal po/_bam po gnyis pa/_de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po nam mkha'i snying po 'khor mang po'i nang nas 'dug pa'i stan las langs te bla gos

[\[43a.7\]]thams cad mkhyen ni bsgrubs pas na/_/sems can byang chub yan lag ldan mdzad nas/_/mthar ni yon tan rgya mtsho phyogs par mdzad/_/gser 'od dam pa'i rnga chen sgra 'di ni/_/thos pa kun ni tshangs dbyangs thob par shog_/bla med byang chub 'bras bu 
[\[43b\]]
[\[43b.1\]]thob gyur nas/_/chos kyi 'khor lo gya nom rtag bskor shog_/tshe ni bskal pa bsam mi khyab gnas nas/_/ji bzhin chos bshad skye dgu phan byed shog_/nyon mongs sdug bsngal mang po rgyun bcad nas/_/'dod sdang gti mug la sogs zhi bar shog_/sems 
[\[43b.2\]]can gang zhig ngan 'gro gnas gyur nas/_/me chen rab 'bar lus kun khyab pa dag_/rnga sgra dam pa 'di ni thos gyur na/_/sdug bsngal bral nas sangs rgyas skyabs 'gror shog_/thams cad sngon gnas shes pa grub gyur nas/_/'das pa'i tshe rabs brgya stong 
[\[43b.3\]]dran par shog_/thub pa'i gtso bo yang dag dran gyur nas/_/de bzhin gshegs bstan zab mo thos par shog_/gser gyi rnga dbyangs dam pa thos pa'i dbang /_/rtag par sangs rgyas rnams ni nyer bsten nas/_/thams cad sdig pa'i las ni yongs spangs 
[\[43b.4\]]te/_/rnam dag dge phyogs 'ba' zhig spyod par shog_/lha dang mi yi srog chags thams cad kun/_/gus pa'i smon lam rtse gcig 'debs pa dag_/gser gyi rnga sgra dam pa thos gyur nas/_/bsam pa rnams ni yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur/_/
[\[43b.5\]]sems can gang zhig mnar med lhung gyur nas/_/dmyal ba'i 'bar mes lus ni nyon mongs te/_/mgon skyabs med cing 'khor bar gang gnas pa/_/thos par gyur na sdug bsngal zhi bar 'gyur/_/lha mi [{]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]dud 'gro'i skye gnas su/_/sdug 
[\[43b.6\]]bsngal bgegs mang mngon du myong 'gyur ba/_/gser gyi rnga dbyangs dam pa thos gyur na/_/sdug bsngal spangs nas thar pa thob par 'gyur/_/da ltar phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten na/_/rtag tu bzhugs pa rkang gnyis gtso/_/thugs rje chen po thugs 
[\[43b.7\]]kyis ni/_/brtse bas bdag la dgongs su gsol/_/sems can gang zhig skyabs ma mchis/_/mgon ni bgyid pa ma mchis pa/_/de lta bu yi rnam pa la/_/skyabs chen dag ni bgyid par shog_/bdag gis sngon ni bgyis pa'i sdig/_/las ngan shin tu 
[\[44a\]]
[\[44a.1\]]lci ba rnams/_/deng bdag stobs bcu'i spyan sngar ni/_/thams cad rtse gcig rab tu bshags/_/bdag gis sangs rgyas yid ma ches/_/bla ma gnyen bshes ma gus dang /_/dge ba mang po ma spyad pas/_/rtag tu las ngan bgyis pa'am/_/
[\[44a.2\]]bdag gis mtho btsun rus rigs dang /_/longs spyod dag ni snyems pa dang /_/dar la bab pas dregs gyur nas/_/rtag tu las ngan bgyis pa'am/_/rtag tu log pa'i bsam bskyed nas/_/ngag gis tshig ngan smras pa dang /_/kha na ma tho ma mthong nas/_/rtag tu las ngan 
[\[44a.3\]]bgyis pa 'am/_/rtag tu byis pa'i blo spyad nas/_/ma rig mun pas sems g.yogs dang /_/mi dge'i grogs po'i rjes 'brangs nas/_/rtag tu las ngan bgyis pa 'am/_/rtsed mo dga' ba rnams kyi rgyus/_/mya ngan dag dang ldan 'gyur zhing /_/'dod chags 
[\[44a.4\]]zhe sdang dkris pas na/_/de slad sdig rnams bgyis pa 'am/_/mi dge'i grogs po bsten pa dang /_/phrag dog ser sna bsam pa dang /_/dbul phongs gya gyu spyad pas na/_/de slad sdig rnams bgyis pa 'am/_/sdig mang dag la mi mos kyang /_/'jigs skrag dag dang bcas 
[\[44a.5\]]pa'am/_/rang dbang dag ni ma mchis pas/_/de slad sdig rnams bgyis pa 'am/_/rnam par g.yeng ba'i bsam pa dang /_/khong khro 'khon du 'dzin pa dang /_/bkres skom dag gis gdungs pa'i rgyus/_/de slad sdig rnams bgyis pa 'am/_/bza' dang btung 
[\[44a.6\]]dang gos dag dang /_/bud med dag la chags srid pa'i/_/nyon mongs mes ni bsregs pas na/_/de slad sdig rnams bgyis pa 'am/_/sangs rgyas chos dang dge 'dun la/_/gus pa'i sems ni ma skyed pa'i/_/'di 'dra'i sdig mang gang bgyis pa/_/deng 
[\[44a.7\]]bdag thams cad so sor bshags/_/rang rgyal byang chub sems dpa' la/_/gus pa'i sems ni ma mchis pa'i/_/'di 'dra'i sdig mang gang bgyis pa/_/deng bdag thams cad so sor bshags/_/mi shes dam chos skur pa dang /_/pha ma sri zhu ma 
[\[44b\]]
[\[44b.1\]]bgyis pa'i/_/de 'dra'i sdig mang gang bgyis pa/_/deng bdag thams cad so sor bshags/_/gti mug dang ni nga rgyal dang /_/'dod chags zhe sdang mthu yis ni/_/de 'dra'i sdig mang gang bgyis pa/_/deng bdag thams cad so sor bshags/_/bdag 
[\[44b.2\]]gis phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten khams/_/sangs rgyas grangs med gang mchod pa/_/sems can rnams ni rab drangs nas/_/sdug bsngal bgegs rnams bral bgyid shog_/sems can dag ni thams cad kun/_/sa bcu la ni gnas gyur te/_/bsod 
[\[44b.3\]]nams ye shes yongs rdzogs nas/_/mngon sangs skye dgu 'dren par shog_/bdag gis sems can kun gyi slad/_/bskal ba brgya stong dka' spyad nas/_/shes rab chen po'i stobs kyis ni/_/sdug bsngal rgya mtsho mthar bgyid shog_/bdag gis 
[\[44b.4\]]srog chags kun gyi slad/_/gser 'od mchog tu rnam par rgyal/_/zab mo'i mdo sde bshad nas ni/_/las ngan rnams ni bsal bar shog_/gang zhig bskal pa brgya stong du/_/sdig lci dag ni bgyis pa rnams/_/skad cig tsam zhig mthol bshags 
[\[44b.5\]]na/_/sdig pa mang po ma lus byang /_/gser 'od dam pa 'di bzhin du/_/de ltar bshags pa bgyis gyur na/_/sdug bsngal las mang thams cad kun/_/'di yi dbang gis myur du 'byang /_/ting 'dzin mchog ni brgya stong dang /_/bsam gyis mi 
[\[44b.6\]]khyab gzungs rnams dang /_/dbang stobs byang chub lam yan lag_/rtag tu sgom yang skyo mi 'gyur/_/bdag gis rin chen phun tshogs nas/_/sa bcu la ni phyin gyur nas/_/sangs rgyas yon tan yongs rdzogs te/_/'khor bar 'bab pa sgrol bar shog_/
[\[44b.7\]]bdag gis sangs rgyas mtsho las ni/_/shin tu zab mo yon tan mdzod/_/gya nom ye shes bsam dka' ba/_/phun sum tshogs pa thob par shog_/phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas thams cad kun/_/bdag la so sor gzigs dgongs nas/_/thugs rje chen po'i 
[\[45a\]]
[\[45a.1\]]thugs kyis ni/_/bdag gis bshags pa bzhes su gsol/_/bdag gis bskal pa mang por ni/_/las ngan mang po bgyis pas na/_/de yi dbang gis sdug bsngal myong /_/thugs rjes byang bar mdzad par smon/_/bdag gis las ngan mang 
[\[45a.2\]]bgyis pa/_/rtag tu mya ngan 'jigs sems skyes/_/spyod lam bzhi po kun tu yang /_/dga' ba'i 'du shes yod ma mchis/_/sangs rgyas thugs rje chen po ldan/_/sems can 'jigs pa sel mdzad na/_/bdag gis bshags pa bzhes nas ni/_/mya 
[\[45a.3\]]ngan sdug bsngal bral bar smon/_/bdag la nyon mongs sgrib pa dang /_/rnam smin las ni ci mchis pa/_/thugs rje chen po'i chab kyis ni/_/rab bkrus byang bar mdzad par smon/_/bdag gis sngon chad sdig pa dang /_/da ltar las ngan bgyis pa rnams/_/
[\[45a.4\]]rtse gcig sems kyis mnol bshags na/_/thams cad rab tu byang bar shog_/ma 'ongs pa yi las ngan rnams/_/bsdams te slong bar yong mi bgyid/_/gal te 'gal ba mchis na yang /_/nams kyang 'chab par yong mi bgyid/_/lus gsum 
[\[45a.5\]]ngag gi rnam pa bzhi/_/yid kyi las ni rnam pa gsum/_/sems can rnams ni 'ching bgyid pas/_/thog ma med nas rgyun mi 'chad/_/spyod pa 'di gsum dbang gis na/_/mi dge las bcu bgyis gyur pas/_/de 'dra'i nongs pa mang po rnams/_/de dag 
[\[45a.6\]]thams cad so sor bshags/_/bdag gis las ngan mang bgyis pas/_/sdug bsngal rnam smin rang nyid myong /_/sangs rgyas rnams kyi spyan sngar ni/_/bsam pa thag pas so sor bshags/_/'dzam bu'i gling ni 'di dag dang /_/'jig rten khams ni gzhan

[\[45b\]]
[\[45b.1\]]lus dang ngag yid kyis/_bsod nams ye shes las bsgrubs pa'i/_/dge ba'i rtsa ba 'di yi mthus/_/bla med ye shes myur grub shog_/deng bdag stobs bcu'i spyan sngar mngon sum du/_/sdug bsngal bgegs mang sna tshogs mthol zhing bshags/_/byis pa 
[\[45b.2\]]srid gsum rmongs pa'i bgegs dag dang /_/rtag par las ngan lci bar bgyis pa'i bgegs/_/bdag gis log pa'i 'dod pas bsags pa'i bgegs/_/rtag tu srid bskyed 'khor bar 'gyur ba'i bgegs/_/'jig rten 'di la mngon zhen chags pa'i bgegs/_/
[\[45b.3\]]byis pa thams cad kun gyi nyon mongs bgegs/_/sems myos rnam g.yeng phyin ci log pa'i bgegs/_/mi dge grogs po dag la brten pa'i bgegs/_/'khor ba dag la 'dod chags zhen pa'i bgegs/_/zhe sdang gti mug glen pas sdig bgyis bgegs/_/
[\[45b.4\]]mi khom brgyad skye mi dge grogs po'i bgegs/_/bsod nams yon tan ma bsags pa yi bgegs/_/deng bdag rgyal ba kun gyi spyan sngar ni/_/sdig pa'i las ngan mtha' yas bgyis pa bshags/_/deng bdag bde gshegs kun la skyabs su mchi/_/
[\[45b.5\]]bla med yon tan mtsho la bdag phyag 'tshal/_/gser gyi ri ltar phyogs bcu gsal mdzad pa/_/byams dang thugs rjes bdag ni gzung du gsol/_/sku ni gser mdog rnam dag dri mi mnga'/_/spyan ni rnam dag bai DUr+ya dang 'dra/_/dpal dang gzi brjid 
[\[45b.6\]]grags dang ldan pa'i gtso/_/thugs rje shes rab nyi mas mun sel ba/_/sangs rgyas nyi ma'i 'od kyis kun gsal mdzad/_/shin tu rnam dag dri med rdul dang bral/_/thub pa'i zla bas gsal mdzad rab bsil ba/_/sems can nyon mongs gdung ba sel bar

[\[46a\]]
[\[46a.1\]]ltar nam mkha' dag la bzhugs/_/shel mchog dam pa'i dra bas gser sku bsgribs/_/'od zer sna tshogs dag gis rab tu brgyan/_/'khor ba'i sdug bsngal chu bo'i nang du ni/_/rga nad mya ngan chu yis ded pas na/_/de 'dra'i sdug bsngal rgya mtsho bzod dka' ba/_/
[\[46a.2\]]sangs rgyas nyi ma'i 'od kyis gtan du bskams/_/deng bdag stong gsum 'jig rten rmad byung gtso/_/thams cad mkhyen pa spyi btud phyag 'tshal lo/_/'od zer rab gsal gser mdog 'dra ba'i sku/_/dpe byad dam pa sna tshogs dag gis brgyan/_/ji ltar rgya mtsho'i chu tshad 
[\[46a.3\]]shes dka' zhing /_/sa chen rdul ni bgrang du ma mchis dang /_/ji ltar ri rab srang grangs gzhal dka' zhing /_/nam mkha' dag la mu mtha' ma mchis ltar/_/sangs rgyas rnams kyi yon tan de bzhin te/_/sems can thams cad kun gyis rig mi 'gyur/_/bskal pa

[\[46a.5\]]sems can thams cad lhan cig 'dus nas ni/_/bcom ldan grags pa'i chos ni thams cad dang /_/mtshan bzang rnam dag dkon mchog bsngags brjod kyang /_/de yi pha mtha' dpag par yongs mi 'gyur/_/bdag gi dge ba'i las mang ci mchis pas/_/myur 
[\[46a.6\]]du bla na ma mchis gtso grub nas/_/yang dag chos bshad skye dgu phan btags te/_/thams cad sdug bsngal mang las thar bgyid shog_/bdud kyi dmag tshogs stobs chen rab btul nas/_/bla med dam chos 'khor lo bskor bar shog_/bskal pa grangs med bsam mi

[\[46b\]]
[\[46b.1\]]gis rtag tu tshe rabs dran gyur nas/_/'das pa'i skye ba brgya stong dran pa dang /_/rtag par thub pa'i gtso bo dran gyur te/_/sangs rgyas zab mo'i chos ni thos par shog_/bdag gi dge ba'i las ni 'di rnams kyis/_/rgyal ba tshad med dag la bsnyen bkur nas/_/mi dge ba yi

[\[46b.4\]]de yis sdug bsngal mi bzad myong ba'i tshe/_/mgon skyabs dag ni bgyid pa ma mchis dang /_/gang dag gzhu brdeg mtshon chas bcings pa dang /_/gnod byed yo byad sna tshogs lus nyon mongs/_/mya ngan brgya stong tshad med myong ba na/_/lus sems rab gzer bde ba cung 
[\[46b.5\]]zad med/_/de dag thams cad bcings shing brdegs pa dang /_/sdug bsngal nyon mongs dag las thar bar shog_/bsad la thug pa dag ni srog thob nas/_/sdug bsngal mang po thams cad gtan zad shog_/sems can gang zhig bkres skom gdungs gyur pa/_/ro 
[\[46b.6\]]mchog dam pa sna tshogs 'thob par shog_/long bas gzugs mthong 'on pas sgra thos dang /_/thing pos 'gro nus lkugs pa smra nus shog_/dbul phongs sems can rin chen gter thob nas/_/bang mdzod rab rgyas rgud pa ma mchis te/_/thams cad bde mchog dam pa

[\[47a\]]
[\[47a.1\]]de dag rol mo bsams pa bzhin/_/sgra mchog mang po mngon du 'byung bar shog_/chu 'dod gser gyi me tog steng 'phyo ba/_/rab bsil rdzing bu mngon du snang bar shog_/sems can de dag ji ltar bsams pa bzhin/_/bza' btung gos dang khri dang stan

[\[47a.3\]]sems kyis brtse bgyid shog_/'jig rten bde ba'i yo byad longs spyod rnams/_/yid la bsams tshe thams cad 'byor par shog_/nor rnams rnyed pa phongs pa ma mchis par/_/bgo bsha' bgyis te sems can gtong bar shog_/bdug spos phye ma dang ni byug spos dang /_/
[\[47a.4\]]me tog sna tshogs kha dog gcig min pa/_/nyin re dus gsum shing las lhung gyur nas/_/yid bzhin longs spyod dga' bskyed par shog_/sems can thams cad phyogs bcu'i rgyal gtso dang /_/theg gsum rnam dag gya nom chos kyi sku/_/byang chub sems

[\[47a.6\]]phyug po dag gi khyim skyes te/_/nor dang rin chen bang mdzod rgyas pa dang /_/byed bzhin grags pa dpe zla ma mchis te/_/tshe ring bskal pa grangs med gnas par shog_/bud med thams cad kun ni phor gyur nas/_/dpa' zhing sems rno blo gros shin tu che/_/thams 
[\[47a.7\]]cad byang chub sems dpa'i lam spyad nas/_/pha rol phyin drug sgrub la brtson par shog_/rtag par phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas tshad med snyed/_/rin chen shing rgyal drung du gshegs nas ni/_/bai DUr+ya yi gdan la rab bzhugs te/_/chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba mthong bar 
[\[47b\]]
[\[47b.1\]]shog_/gal te 'das pa dang ni da ltar tshe/_/srid gsum 'khor nas las rnams bgyis pas na/_/yid du mi mchi ngan 'gro thob gyur pa/_/thams cad ma lus gtan du byang bar shog_/sems can thams cad srid pa'i rgya mtshor ni/_/'khor ba'i zhags pas dam du

[\[47b.4\]]pa dang /_/bsam pa dang zhing rnyog pa ma mchis pas/_/bsngo zhing smon lam btab pa'i bsod nams des/_/bskal pa drug cu'i ngan 'gro 'das par smon/_/rigs kyi bu dang rigs kyi bu mo dang /_/bram ze la sogs rigs mchog dam pa rnams/_/thal sbyar rtse 
[\[47b.5\]]gcig sangs rgyas bsngags brjod na/_/skye ba rtag par kun du tshe rabs dran/_/dbang po tshang zhing lus ni yongs rdzogs te/_/yon tan dam pa thams cad phun tshogs 'gyur/_/ma 'ongs pa yi skye ba thams cad du/_/rtag par lha mi rnams kyis blta bar shog_/
[\[47b.6\]]sangs rgyas gcig dang sangs rgyas bcu dag las/_/dge ba'i rtsa ba bsgrubs nas thob pa min/_/sangs rgyas brgya stong dge rtsa bskyed pas na/_/'gyod tshangs chos 'di thos par gyur pa yin/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de skad ces gsol pa gsan pa dang /_

[\[48a\]]
[\[48a.1\]]mthong ba gang yin pa de gang la la zhig gis thos par gyur na bsod nams shin tu mang po 'thob par 'gyur zhing sems can la rgya cher phan 'dogs pa dang /_kha na ma tho ba'i sgrib pa yongs su byang bar 'gyur ro/_/khyod kyi las mchog dam pa 'di dag thams cad 
[\[48a.2\]]ni 'das pa'i dus su bsngags pa brjod cing smon lam btab pa'i sngon gyi bag chags kyi rgyu rkyen dang /_de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyis byin gyis brlabs pa'i mthus rgyu rkyen 'di dag khyod la bstan pa yin par rig par bya'o/_/de'i tshe 'khor mang po rnams de skad ces 
[\[48a.3\]]bka' stsal pa thos nas thams cad rab tu dga' zhing mngon par yid ches nas bka' bzhin du spyod par gyur to/_/gser 'od dam pa mchog tu rnam par rgyal ba mdo sde'i rgyal po las rmi lam na 'gyod tshangs byed pa mthong ba'i le'u ste bzhi pa'o//_//gser 'od 
[\[48a.4\]]dam pa mchog tu rnam par rgyal ba mdo sde'i rgyal po/_bam po gsum pa/_de nas bcom ldan 'das yang dag pa'i rnam par rtog pa la bzhugs te shin tu zab cing gya nom pa'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs pa dang /_sku'i spu'i bu gnas kha dog rnam pa sna tshogs brgya stong 
[\[48a.5\]]tshad med pa snyed dang ldan pa'i 'od zer chen po bkye bas phyogs bcu'i gang gA'i klung gi bye ma dag gi tshad dang dper byar med pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing rnams 'od zer gyis rab tu snang bar gyur to/_/snyigs ma lnga'i 'jig rten dag tu 'od zer gyis snang bar byas pas sems can gang 
[\[48a.6\]]dag mi dge ba bcu'i las dang /_mtshams med pa lnga'i kha na ma tho ba dang /_dkon mchog gsum la skur pa btab pa dang /_bla ma dang nye ba dag la sri zhu ma byas pa dang /_mkhan po dang bram ze dag la brnyas shing nga rgyal byas pas sems can dmyal ba dang /[ {]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]
[\[48a.7\]]dang /_dud 'gro'i skye gnas su ltung bar gyur pa de dag so so nas 'od zer gyi snang ba de dag gi gnas su phyin par gyur pas sems can de dag 'od zer de mthong ba dang /_'od zer gyi mthus thams cad kyis bde ba dang /_shin tu sdug cing mdzes pa'i gzugs kyis mtshan

[\[48b.1\]]phun sum tshogs pa dang /_bsod nams dang ye shes kyis brgyan nas sangs rgyas rnams mthong bar gyur to/_/de'i tshe brgya byin dang /_lha'i 'khor thams cad dang /_gang gA'i lha mo dang /_'khor mang po rnams 'od zer rmad du byung ba mthong ba dang /_thams cad bcom ldan 
[\[48b.2\]]'das kyi spyan sngar lhags te g.yas phyogs su lan gsum bskor ba byas nas phyogs gcig tu 'khod do/_/de nas brgya byin lha'i dbang po bcom ldan 'das kyis byin gyis brlabs kyi mthus stan las langs te/_bla gos phrag pa gcig tu gzar nas/_pus mo g.yas pa'i 
[\[48b.3\]]lha nga sa la btsugs te/_bcom ldan 'das ga la ba de logs su thal mo sbyar nas/_bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das rigs kyi bu'am/_rigs kyi bu mo gang la la zhig gis bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub gnyer zhing

[\[48b.5\]]'das kyis brgya byin lha'i dbang po la bka' stsal pa/_rigs kyi bu deng khyed kyis tshad med dpag tu med pa'i sems can rnams rnal 'byor bsgom nas rnam par dag pa'i rnam par grol ba'i bde ba 'thob par bya ba dang /_'jig rten thams cad la snying brtse zhing phan par 'dod 
[\[48b.6\]]pa legs so legs so/_/sems can gang la la zhig las kyi sgrib pa'i dbang gis sdig pa rnams mngon par 'dus byas pa gang yin pa des nan tan brtson 'grus bskyed de nyin mtshan dus drug tu/_bla gos phrag pa gcig tu gzar te/_pus mo g.yas pa'i lha nga sa la

[\[52a.1\]]bcom ldan 'das 'jig rten na skyes pa dang /_bud med ji snyed mchis pa dag las theg pa chen po'i spyod pa spyod par rngo thogs pa yang mchis spyod par rngo mi thogs pa yang mchis na ji ltar sems can thams cad kyi bsod nams kyi dge ba'i rtsa ba dag la rjes su yi rang

[\[53a.1\]]'gro'o/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_mchod pa byas pa'i bsod nams gang yin pa de ni grangs dang bcas shing tshad dang bcas pas bsod nams thams cad yongs su sdud par mi 'gyur la rjes su yi rang ba'i bsod nams gang yin pa de la ni tshad med cing grangs

[\[53a.3\]]su bsgyur nas skyes pa'i lus yongs su 'grub par 'dod na yang rjes su yi rang ba'i bsod nams mngon par sgrub par byed na gdon mi za bar yid la bsams pa bzhin du tshe 'di nyid la skyes pa'i lus mngon par 'grub par 'gyur ro/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das la brgya byin

[\[53a.5\]]dag pa'i rnal 'byor sgom par 'gyur bar bgyid pa'i slad du bskul ba dang /_gsol ba btab pa'i bsod nams gang lags pa bshad du gsol/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_lha'i dbang po rigs kyi bu'am rigs kyi bu mo gang la la zhig bla na med pa

[\[63a.1\]]par nus pa dang /_byams pa dang /_snying rje'i bsam pa bskyed nas sems can gyi dge ba'i rtsa ba rnams yongs su smin par byed pa dang /_mi skye ba'i chos kyi bzod pa zab mo thob par gyur pa gang yin pa ste/_rigs kyi bu 'di ni byang chub sems dpa'

[\[82a\]]
[\[82a.1\]]'das byang chub sems dpa' yid bzhin rin chen 'od 'phro 'di ni bsam gyis mi khyab pas don zab mo 'di lta bu mngon par rtogs par 'gyur lags so/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_de de bzhin no/_/de de bzhin te/_tshangs pa'i rgyal po khyod 
[\[82a.2\]]kyis ji skad smras pa de bzhin du yid bzhin rin chen 'od 'phro 'dis bstan nas khyed mi skye ba'i chos kyi bzod pa sgom zhing bslab par sems bskyed pa yin no/_/de nas tshangs pa'i rgyal po chen po tshangs pa'i 'khor mang po dang lhan cig tu stan las langs te bla gos phrag pa gcig la 
[\[82a.3\]]gzar nas gus pas thal mo sbyar te byang chub sems dpa' yid bzhin rin chen 'od 'phro'i rkang pa la mgo bos phyag byas nas 'di skad du shin tu dkon zhing ngo mtshar che'o/_/bdag cag ni deng skyes bu chen po 'di dang phrad pas yang dag pa'i chos thos par gyur to/_/zhes smras so/_/
[\[82a.4\]]de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis tshangs pa'i rgyal po la 'di skad ces yid bzhin rin chen 'od 'phro 'di ni ma 'ongs pa na de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rig pa dang zhabs su ldan pa/_bde bar gshegs pa/_'jig rten mkhyen pa/_
[\[82a.5\]]bla na med pa'i skyes bu 'dul ba'i kha lo sgyur ba/_lha dang mi rnams kyi ston pa sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rin chen 'bar ba dpal gyi snying po zhes bya bar 'gyur ro zhes bka' stsal to/_/le'u 'di bstan pa na byang chub sems dpa' 'bum phrag sum stong bla na med pa yang dag 
[\[82a.6\]]par rdzogs pa'i byang chub las phyir mi ldog pa thob par gyur to/_/lha'i bu 'bum phrag brgyad stong dang /_rgyal po dang /_blon po dang /_mi phal pa tshad med grangs med pa snyed ni rdul spangs shing dri ma dang bral nas chos kyi mig rnam par dag pa thob par gyur to/_/de nas 
[\[82a.7\]]de'i tshe 'khor mang po de'i nang nas dge slong 'bum phrag lnga bcu byang chub sems dpa'i spyad pa spyod pa las byang chub kyi sems las phyir mi ldog pa rnams byang chub sems dpa' yid bzhin rin chen 'od 'phros chos bstan pa thos pas thams cad shin tu brtan par gyur 
[\[82b\]]
[\[82b.1\]]nas bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i smon lam mchog yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur bas yang byang chub tu sems bskyed nas/_so so nas rang gi gos phud de byang chub sems dpa' la mchod pa byas nas mchog tu brtson pa'i sems bla na med pa bskyed de/_smon lam 'di skad du bdag 
[\[82b.2\]]cag bsod nams kyi dge ba'i rtsa ba ci bsags pa thams cad bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub las phyir mi ldog par yongs su bsngo'o/_/zhes smon lam btab bo/_/tshangs pa'i rgyal po dge slong mang po 'di dag ni bsod nams 'di la brten nas ji skad bstan 
[\[82b.3\]]pa bzhin du mngon par bsgrubs pas bskal pa chen po dgu bcu 'das nas khong du chud cing go bar 'gyur bas 'khor ba las nges par 'byang bar 'gyur ro/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis dge slong mang po de dag la khyed bskal pa grangs med pa sum cu 'das nas mngon 
[\[82b.4\]]par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar 'gyur te/_bskal pa'i ming ni rgyal bar dka' ba'i 'od kyi rgyal po zhes bya'o/_/zhing gi ming ni dri ma med pa'i 'od ces bya ste/_dus gcig tu bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar 'gyur bas 
[\[82b.5\]]thams cad ming mthun par smon lam gyi bkod pas brgyan pa'i rgyal po zhes bya bar 'gyur zhing mtshan rnam pa bcu dang ldan par 'gyur ro zhes lung bstan to/_/tshangs pa'i rgyal po gser 'od dam pa shin tu gya nom pa'i mdo sde 'di gang gis yang dag par mnyan nas 
[\[82b.6\]]yongs su 'dzin par byed na gzi byin chen po'i stobs dang ldan par 'gyur bas gang la la zhig gis bskal pa chen po brgya stong du ma'i bar du thabs dang mi ldan par pha rol tu phyin pa drug yongs su spyad pa las rigs kyi bu'am rigs kyi bu mo gang la la zhig gis gser 
[\[82b.7\]]'od dam pa'i mdo sde 'di yi ger bris nas zla ba phyed phyed cing sems rtse gcig tu klog gam/_kha ton du 'don par byed na 'di'i bsod nams gang yin pa de snga ma'i bsod nams dang bskrun na brgya'i char yang nye bar mi 'gro ba nas bgrang ba dang dpe dang rgyu'i bar du yang nye bar 
[\[83a\]]
[\[83a.1\]]mi 'gro'o/_/tshangs pa'i rgyal po de'i phyir ngas deng khyod kyis bslab par bya zhing dran pa yongs su bzung nas gzhan dag la rgya cher bstan par bya'o zhes bstan pa yin no/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_ngas sngon byang chub sems dpa'i lam yongs su spyad pa na ji ltar 
[\[83a.2\]]dpa' bo g.yul ngor 'jug pa bzhin du lus dang srog la phangs pa med par 'di ltar mdo sde'i rgyal po mchog dam pa 'di yongs su spel zhing yang dag par bzung bklags kha ton du bton nas gzhan dag la yang dag par bstan te tshangs pa'i rgyal po 'di lta ste dper na 'khor los 
[\[83a.3\]]sgyur ba'i rgyal po gal te 'jig rten na 'dug par gyur na rin po che sna bdun yang nub par mi 'gyur la/_gal te rgyal po tshe zad par gyur na rin po che sna bdun yod pa dag kyang de nyid du nub cing zad par 'gyur ro/_/tshangs pa'i rgyal po de bzhin du gser 'od dam pa mchog 
[\[83a.4\]]tu rnam par rgyal ba mdo sde'i rgyal po 'di gal te 'jig rten na gnas par gyur na ni bla na med pa'i chos kyi rin po che rnams kyang nub par mi 'gyur la/_gal te mdo sde 'di med par gyur na ni gnas de nyid du nub par 'gyur ro/_/de lta bas na mdo sde'i rgyal po 'di sems rtse 
[\[83a.5\]]gcig tu mnyan nas yongs su gzung bar bya/_bklag par bya/_kha ton du gdon par bya/_gzhan dag la bstan par bya zhing yi ger 'dri bar bskul nas brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa spyad de/_lus dang srog la yang phangs pa med par rab tu brtson par byed na bsod nams 
[\[83a.6\]]kyi nang na mchog tu 'gyur bas nga'i slob ma rnams kyis de bzhin du rab tu brtson pas rnal 'byor bsgom par bslab par bya'o/_/de nas tshangs pa'i rgyal po chen po tshangs pa'i 'khor tshad med pa snyed dang /_brgya byin dang /_rgyal po chen po bzhi dang /_gnod sbyin rnams dang lhan 
[\[83a.7\]]cig tu stan las langs te bla gos phrag pa gcig la gzar nas pus mo g.yas pa'i lha nga sa la btsugs te/_gus pas thal mo sbyar nas bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das bdag cag gis gser 'od dam 
[\[83b\]]
[\[83b.1\]]pa mchog tu rnam par rgyal ba'i mdo sde 'di dang /_chos 'chad pa'i mkhan po gang lags pa de dag yongs su bsrung zhing spel bar bgyid la/_gal te bgegs bgyid pa zhig mchis na yang bdag cag gis yongs su bsal nas dge ba mang po dang ldan pa dang /_kha 
[\[83b.2\]]dog dang /_mthu stobs dang ldan pa dang /_spobs pa la thogs pa ma mchis pa dang /_lus dang sems bag yangs su 'gyur ba dang /_nyan pa'i 'khor rnams bde ba myong bar bgyid do/_/yul gang na gnas pa der mu ge dang /_phyir rgol ba'i dgra dang /_mi

[\[83b.5\]]po dang /_tshangs pa'i 'khor rnams nas rgyal po chen po bzhi dang /_gnod sbyin la sogs pa'i bar dag la bka' stsal pa legs so legs so/_/khyed kyis chos zab mo gya nom pa thos nas mdo sde'i rgyal po mchog dam pa 'di dang mdo sde 'dzin pa dag la yongs su bsrungs shing yongs su

[\[84a.1\]]de nas lha'i rgyal po rnam thos kyi bu dang /_lha'i rgyal po yul 'khor srung dang /_lha'i rgyal po 'phags skyes po dang /_lha'i rgyal po mig mi bzang cig char stan las langs te/_bla gos phrag pa gcig la gzar nas pus mo g.yas pa'i lha nga sa la btsugs te/_

[\[84b.5\]]'das rgyu dang rkyen des na bdag cag rgyal po rnams kyang 'jig rten skyong ba zhes bgyi ba lags so/_/gzhan yang 'dzam bu'i gling 'dir yul gyi rgyal po gang la la zhig la phas kyi rgol ba'i dgra rnams rtag par nye bar mchis te gnod pa bgyis par gyur tam/_mu ge dang rims nad

[\[85a.6\]]rgyal po des ci dang ci 'tshal ba sbyar te/_mchod pa bgyis nas phongs pa ma mchis par bgyid par gyur na bdag cag rgyal po chen po bzhis yul gyi bdag po de dang yul gyi mi thams cad rab tu bde bar bgyid cing gnod pa rnams thag ring du spong bar bgyid do/_/bcom ldan 'das

[\[86a.1\]]dang /_lha'i 'khor gzhan rnams dang /_gnod sbyin rnams dang /_lha ma yin dang 'thab pa'i tshe rtag tu rgyal ba'i phan yon 'thob par 'gyur ro/_/khyed cag mdo sde 'di yongs su bsrung zhing yongs su 'dzin par byed na mdo sde 'di'i mthus sdug bsngal mang po

[\[86b.1\]]nas sems can mang po kun gyis bkur bar 'gyur bar bgyid pas/_de'i slad du bdag cag gnod sbyin dang 'dre srin tshad ma mchis pa dang bcas par mdo sde'i rgyal po 'di gang du spel ba'i gnas der lus sbas nas yongs su skyob pa dang /_yongs su srung ba bgyid pas bgegs rnams

[\[86b.3\]]mi'i rgyal po gang la la zhig mdo sde 'di nyan pa'i tshe nye 'khor na gnas pa'i dgra phyir rgol ba dmag rnam pa bzhi bshams te/_yul de dag gzhom mo snyam du sems par gyur na yang bcom ldan 'das 'di ltar mdo sde'i rgyal po 'di'i byin gyi brlabs kyi mthus de'i tshe nye 'khor gyi dgra de la 
[\[86b.4\]]phyir rgol ba'i dgra tha dad pa gzhan mchis nas gzhom par bgyid cing /_de'i yul khams su ltas ngan mang po dang rims nad rnams 'byung bar 'gyur bas de'i tshe rgyal po des de lta bu mthong ba dang /_dmag rnam pa bzhi bshams nas yul der chas te gzhom par bgyid pa na bdag cag

[\[119a.1\]]du gyur nas nam mkha' la mchi bar 'tshal lam/_lha'i mig gi mngon par shes pa dang /_gzhan gyi sems 'tshal ba dang /_sems can thams cad la yid bzhin du rang dbang thob nas nyon mongs pa bcad de myur du rnam par grol ba thob pa la dgod

[\[119b.1\]]'das kyis 'khor mang po'i nang du sa'i lha mo brtan ma la bka' stsal pa/_khyod legs par nyon cig_/sngon 'das pa'i dus kyi tshe rgyal po stobs kyi gtso bo'i tog ces bya ba byung ngo /_/rgyal po de'i bu mdzes pa'i tog ces bya ba zhig yod de/_spyi bo nas dbang

[\[123b.7\]]byas/_/lha dbang lha tshogs lha yi bu mo rnams/_/thams cad me tog man dA ra yang gtor/_/lha yi rol mo brgya stong bsam mi khyab/_/mkha' la gnas nas sgra dbyangs snyan par phyung /_/de tshe chos smra chen po rin chen brtsegs/_/stan la 'dug ste skyil mo krung bcas nas/_/
[\[124a\]]
[\[124a.1\]]phyogs bcu'i zhing na bzhugs pa'i byams chen gtso/_/bye ba brgya stong de dag yid la bsams/_/sdug bsngal sems can thams cad ji snyed pa/_/thams cad byams sems mnyam pa nyer gzhag nas/_/gsol 'debs bdag po rgyal po legs pa'i phyir/_/
[\[124a.2\]]gser 'od dam pa'i mdo sde rab tu bshad/_/rgyal pos de 'dra'i chos ni thos gyur nas/_/thal sbyar rtse gcig rjes su yi rang smras/_/ya mtshan chos thos mchi ma blags te ngus/_/lus dang sems ni dga' chen kun khyab gyur/_/de tshe yul bdag rgyal po legs skyes kyis/_/mdo 
[\[124a.3\]]sde 'di ni mchod par 'dod pa'i phyir/_/lag na yid bzhin nor bu thogs nas ni/_/sems can kun phyir smon lam 'di skad btab/_/'dzam gling 'di ni kun du khyab par yang /_/rin chen rgyan phreng char ni rab phab nas/_/nor dang yo byad rgud phongs ji snyed pa/_/thams cad 
[\[124a.4\]]yid bzhin bde ba myong bar shog_/de nas rin chen sna bdun char phab nas/_/gling bzhi kun du chog cing tshim byas pas/_/lus kyi rgyan phreng gos dang zas skom dang /_/ci dang ci 'dod phongs pa med par gyur/_/de tshe yul bdag rgyal po legs skyes kyis/_/gling 
[\[124a.5\]]bzhi rin chen char bab mthong ba dang /_/thams cad sangs rgyas rin chen gtsug phud kyi/_/bstan la zhugs pa'i dge slong dge 'dun mchod/_/'das pa'i rgyal po legs skyes gang yin pa/_/shAkya thub pa nga yin shes par byos/_/sngon yang sa chen mang po rab 
[\[124a.6\]]btang ste/_/rin chen dag gis gling bzhi yongs su bkang /_/sngon tshe chos mkhan chen po rin chen brtsegs/_/legs skyes de la gya nom chos bshad de/_/mdo sde'i rgyal po de dag bshad pa'i phyir/_/shar phyogs sangs rgyas mi 'khrugs grub pa yin/_/ngas ni mdo sde'i 
[\[124a.7\]]rgyal po 'di mnyan nas/_/thal sbyar rtse gcig yi rang brjod pa dang /_/rin chen sna bdun btang ba'i bsod nams kyis/_/rdo rje sku mchog 'di ni brnyes pa yin/_/gser 'od bsod nams brgya yi mtshan brgyan pas/_/mthong ba thams cad kun ni rab dga' zhing /_/
[\[124b\]]
[\[124b.1\]]sems can thams cad kun gyi snying du sdug_/lha tshogs mang po bye ba kun de bzhin/_/'das tshe bskal pa bye ba dgu bcu dgu/_/'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal po dag kyang byas/_/yul khams chung ngur mi yi rgyal po byas/_/gzhan yang bskal pa brgya stong tshad med snyed/_/brgya 
[\[124b.2\]]byin lha yi dbang po dag kyang byas/_/tshangs pa'i rgyal po chen po dag byas te/_/phyogs bcu'i byams chen gtso la mchod pa yi/_/de yi grangs tshad shin tu mthar thug dka'/_/ngas sngon mdo thos rjes su yi rang dge/_/bsod nams phung po ci yod tshad shes dka'/_/bsod 
[\[124b.3\]]nams de dbang byang chub mngon rtogs nas/_/chos sku yang dag ye shes dam pa brnyes/_/de nas 'khor mang po rnams de skad ces bka' stsal pa thos pas ngo mtshar rmad du gyur nas thams cad kyis mdo sde gser 'od dam pa spel zhing rgyun mi 'chad par spyad par mos par 
[\[124b.4\]]gyur to/_/gser 'od dam pa mchog tu rnam par rgyal ba mdo sde'i rgyal po las rgyal po legs skyes kyi le'u ste nyi shu gcig pa'o//_//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis dpal gyi lha mo chen mo la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to/_/shin tu dang ba'i sems dad pa dang 
[\[124b.5\]]ldan pa'i rigs kyi bu'am/_/rigs kyi bu mo gang la la zhig gis 'das pa dang /_/ma byon pa dang /_da ltar bzhugs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams la mchod pa'i yo byad bsam gyis mi khyab pa dang /_yangs shing rgya che ba dag gis mchod par byed par 'dod pa dang /_dus 
[\[124b.6\]]gsum gyi sangs rgyas kyi spyod yul zab mo shes shing rtogs par 'dod pa gang yin pa des gdon mi za bar sems rtse gcig tu mdo sde'i rgyal po 'di grong khyer ram/_grong rdal lam/_ri 'am/_nags tshal gang yang rung ste/_gnas de dang de dag tu sems can rnams la rgya cher 
[\[124b.7\]]yang dag par bstan nas yongs su spel bar bya'o/_/chos nyan pa gang yin pa de yang rnam par g.yeng ba'i 'du shes spangs nas rna blags te sems rtse gcig tu gzhag par bya'o/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis lha mo de dang 'khor mang po rnams la tshigs su bcad de bka' 
[\[125a\]]
[\[125a.1\]]stsal pa/_gang zhig sangs rgyas mang po la/_/bsam gyis mi khyab mchod pa dang /_/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi/_/spyod yul zab mo shes 'dod na/_/gal te gser 'od dam pa 'di/_/yang dag phye bshad mthong gyur na/_/de yi phyogs su nyer 
[\[125a.2\]]song nas/_/de yi drung du 'gro bar bya/_/mdo 'di bsam gyis mi khyab pas/_/yon tan mang po spyod byed cing /_/sdug bsngal rgya mtsho mtha' med las/_/sems can rnams ni thar bar byed/_/nga yi mdo 'di rnam bltas na/_/thog ma bar dang tha mar dge/_/shin tu 
[\[125a.3\]]zab pas dpag par dka'/_/dpe dag gis ni zla byar med/_/gang gA'i bye ma sa chen rdul/_/rgya mtsho'i chu dang nam mkha' dang /_/ri brag thams cad gang gis kyang /_/cha shas tsam yang dper byar med/_/chos dbyings zab mor 'jug 'dod na/_/mdo 'di sngar ni 
[\[125a.4\]]mnyan par bya/_/chos nyid mchod rten gang na bar/_/zab mo shin tu bde bar gnas/_/mchod rten 'di yi nang dag tu/_/shAkya thub pa nga yis ni/_/yid 'ong sgra dbyangs dam pa yis/_/mdo sde 'di bshad mthong bar 'gyur/_/des na grangs tshad bsam dka' ba'i/_/
[\[125a.5\]]bskal pa bye ba snyed du ni/_/lha dang mi yi nang skyes nas/_/rtag tu bde ba mchog myong 'gyur/_/mdo sde 'di ni mnyan pa yis/_/bdag ni bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i/_/bsod nams phung po 'thob 'gyur zhes/_/de snyam du ni bsam par bya/_/gal te me chen phung po

[\[125a.7\]]gza' skar ma rungs ltas ngan dang /_/byad stems log pa'i gdon la sogs/_/mdo sde 'di ni thos pa'i tshe/_/sdig pa mang po spong bar byed/_/stan mtho mchog ni rab brgyan nas/_/rab gtsang pad+ma 'dra ba la/_/chos smra steng du phyin nas ni/_/klu chen lta bur

[\[125b.1\]]'dug par bya/_/de la bde bar 'dug nas ni/_/zab mo'i mdo 'di bshad par bya/_/yi ger bris nas klog 'dzin dang /_/de yi don ni bshad par bya/_/chos smra stan ni spangs nas ni/_/phyogs gzhan dag tu song gyur kyang /_/khri stan mthon po de las ni/_/

[\[125b.5\]]sel/_/g.yul 'gyed tshe na rtag par rgyal/_/rmi lam ngan pa thams cad med/_/dug dang gnod pa mang po zhi/_/las gsum sdig pa ci byas pa/_/mdo sde'i mthu yis byang bar byed/_/'dzam bu'i gling ni 'di dag kun/_/grags pa kun du rgyas par 'gyur/_/

[\[148a.1\]]gser 'od dam pa mchog tu rnam par rgyal ba mdo sde'i rgyal po las byang chub kyi shing gi lha mos bsngags pa brjod pa'i le'u ste nyi shu dgu pa'o//_//de nas lha mo spobs pa chen mo stan las langs te gus pas thal mo sbyar nas
